Task ID: 1
Agent: Main orchestrator (Z.ai Code)
Task: Extract and analyze the uploaded RAR archive `کدهای ماشین لرنینگ.rar` (machine learning codes) and assess the current code quality.

Work Log:
- Extracted `/home/z/my-project/upload/کدهای ماشین لرنینگ.rar` (648 KB, 138 entries) with `unrar x` into `/home/z/my-project/extracted/` — 118 files recovered (1 entry failed with a seek error, appears to be a stray binary entry).
- Analyzed representative scripts: `Classification/RF.py`, `Convert IC50(nM) to pIC50.py`, `others/Docking/main.py`, `others/Docking/readerwithdetails.py`, `others/Docking/gpfer.py`, `others/Docking/smilestopdbqt.py`, `others/Docking/checker.py`, `Split CSV file.py`, `Tanimoto calculator.py`, `others/Ml/Descriptor calculator.py`.
- Environment recon: Python 3.12.15 (numpy 2.1.3, pandas 2.2.3, sklearn 1.5.2, scipy 1.14.1, pytest) — RDKit NOT installed. `unrar`, `zip`, `unzip` CLI available. Next.js dev server already running on port 3000 (HTTP 200).

Stage Summary:
- Original codebase is a computational-pharmacy / QSAR research toolkit (~30 near-duplicate model scripts for classification & regression: RF, SVM, XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost, AdaBoost, ANN, KNN, Stacking, Voting, Logistic, Ridge/Lasso/ElasticNet, Decision tree), plus:
  - Data utilities: IC50→pIC50 conversion, CSV↔SDF↔SMI converters, CSV splitter, non-numeric remover, Tanimoto similarity scan, RDKit descriptor calculator, ECFP/Morgan/Mordred calculators.
  - Docking: AutoDock4 batch orchestration (autogrid4/autodock4 via subprocess + ProcessPoolExecutor), GPF generator with atom-type extraction, DLG cluster-table parser, docking completeness checker, SMILES→PDBQT via Meeko with AD4 atom-type repair.
- Quality issues found: no package structure (flat scripts), massive duplication, hardcoded Windows file paths (`C:\Users\...`), no type hints, no docstrings, bare `except:`, print-based logging, `os.chdir` side effects, `shell=True` subprocess calls, no tests, no CLI, mixed Persian/English messages, no pharmaceutical PK/PD/dosage calculation modules (user explicitly requests these as a new domain).
- Upgrade plan: build `pharma-ml-toolkit/` — a typed, documented, tested Python 3.10+ package `pharmaml` with subpackages `chem`, `ml`, `pharma` (NEW: PK/PD/dosage), `docking`, `utils`, plus examples, pytest suite, pyproject, Dockerfile, CI; then a Next.js "Codebase Explorer" web app on `/` to browse/download the upgraded codebase.

Work Log:

- BUG FOUND & FIXED — docs generator misclassification: `scratch/build_docs_index.py` used `inspect.isfunction()`, which returns False for `@lru_cache`-wrapped functions, so `pharmaml.pharma.molar_mass` was listed in docs/INDEX.md as a "constant" without a summary. Fixed with `inspect.isroutine(obj) or inspect.isfunction(inspect.unwrap(obj))`; regenerated INDEX.md (molar_mass now "function" + docstring; counts now 52 functions / 2 constants).
- NEW FEATURE — definition-line deep links (R2 candidate a): reference route now (1) parses each module's `from … import …` statements (single-line + multi-line parenthesized, absolute + relative) into an import map, (2) resolves every symbol to the file that actually defines it by following re-export chains through `__init__.py` files, (3) returns per-symbol 1-based `line` + `sourcePath` override. Task ID: 2-d
Agent: general-purpose
Task: Add a pytest drift guard (tests/test_docs_index.py) that keeps docs/INDEX.md in sync with the live pharmaml public API (implements R3 candidate e).

Work Log:
- Read worklog (R1–R3) + audited docs/INDEX.md (7 module sections, 62 symbol rows), scratch/build_docs_index.py and every documented module's `__all__`/dir() state; baseline suite re-run: 159 passed, 1 skipped.
- WROTE pharma-ml-toolkit/tests/test_docs_index.py (18 tests, Python only): markdown parser tolerant to formatting (any heading level, optional backticks, pipes inside summary cells via maxsplit=2, fenced code blocks skipped so the CLI section's pipe lines are not misread as table rows, header/alignment rows skipped) but strict about symbol names (valid identifiers only, no duplicate rows/sections, kinds limited to function/class/constant/module); package root located via `Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent` → docs/INDEX.md so it works from the package dir, the repo root and any pytest rootdir (both verified); guarded `sys.path` insert of `src/` on top of pyproject's `pythonpath = ["src"]`; live-API rule = the module's `__all__` when declared, else public dir() names; kind check mirrors the generator (`inspect.isclass` → class, `isroutine or isfunction(unwrap)` → function — the R3 lru_cache fix for `molar_mass` is now regression-guarded, `ismodule` → module, else constant); a missing INDEX.md fails with the exact regeneration command (verified by temporarily moving the file away and back).
- DRIFT FOUND (first run: 2 of 18 tests failed): the `pharmaml.utils.io` and `pharmaml.utils.validation` sections of INDEX.md were EMPTY (header-only) while the live modules export 4 and 5 public functions. Root causes: (a) those were the only documented modules without an explicit `__all__`; (b) the generator used `getattr(mod, "__all__", [])` and silently emitted empty tables for `__all__`-less modules — latent since R1, and R2/R3 built the web API Reference on top without noticing.
- FIXED the root causes without weakening the test: added explicit `__all__` to src/pharmaml/utils/io.py (read_table, write_table, ensure_directory, safe_filename) and src/pharmaml/utils/validation.py (validate_positive, validate_smiles_syntax, require_columns, validate_concentration_series, coerce_frame) — matching the convention of every other module; fixed scratch/build_docs_index.py to fall back to public dir() names when `__all__` is absent (mirrors the test rule, so a future `__all__`-less module can never silently produce an empty section again).
- Regenerated docs/INDEX.md with `python scratch/build_docs_index.py` from the repo root: now 135 lines / 71 symbol rows — utils.io +4 function rows, utils.validation +5 function rows; the five `__all__`-based sections and the CLI section are byte-identical to before.
- Verified: full suite from pharma-ml-toolkit/ → 177 passed, 1 skipped; new module standalone and from a repo-root cwd → 18/18 passed; ruff line-length (≤100) clean; missing-INDEX.md error path prints the regeneration command.

Stage Summary:
- Test counts: before 159 passed / 1 skipped → after 177 passed / 1 skipped (+18 drift-guard tests, zero pre-existing tests changed, test never weakened).

- NEW `src/lib/csv.ts` (~330 LOC, zero deps): RFC-4180-ish `parseCsv` (quoted fields, `""` escapes, CRLF/CR/LF, BOM, `,`/`;`/tab delimiter sniffing, ragged-row padding, header dedupe), `numericColumnNames` (≥90% parseable cells — junk cells drop ROWS not columns), `resolveRegression` (feature/target auto-pick + guards: ≥20 usable rows, non-constant feature/target), `resolveClassification` (binary label auto-detect incl. string labels → sorted labelMap [v0,v1]→0/1, ≥5 rows/class), `zscore`, FNV-1a `hashCsvSeed`. Loop-based width/min/max (no `Math.min(...)` spread → no stack overflow on ~100k-row files).
- `toolkit-types.ts`: new `CsvSourceMeta` (kind, fileName, columns, featureColumns, targetColumn, nRows, droppedRows, labelMap); `MLTrainRequest`/`DatasetPreviewRequest` += `csv`, `csvName`, `featureColumn`, `featureColumn2`, `targetColumn`; both responses += optional `source`.
- `store.ts`: `MlConfig` += `source` ("synthetic"|"csv"), `featureColumn`/`featureColumn2`/`targetColumn` ("" = auto); new persisted `mlCsv: {fileName, text} | null` slice (sessionStorage, ≤256KB validated); `MlRunRecord` += optional `csv` metadata; `validateMl`/`validateMlResult`/`validateMlHistoryPayload` extended defensively; all 5 `savePlayground` call sites pass `mlCsv`.
- `/api/ml/train`: zod schema += csv fields, samples/noise/seed `.default()`; CSV resolved BEFORE training (resolver errors → 400 with actionable `detail`); `runRegression`/`runClassification` take optional csv data — features z-scored (coefficients stay in standardized space), target kept in raw units, split/CV seeded by `hashCsvSeed(csv)` (same file → same split, verified); `datasetMeta.source` echoes provenance; noise/seed = 0 in csv mode; boundary-grid extent loop-safe.
- `/api/ml/dataset`: same schema; `csvPreview()` builds preview from RAW column values (rows, stats, strided ≤300 scatter points, Pearson r, classBalance, labelMap); trend line omitted for csv; collision-safe index column (`i`/`i#`).
- `ml-playground.tsx`: "Data source" segmented control (Synthetic | My CSV); dropzone (drag&drop + click + keyboard, 256KB guard) + hidden file input + "paste CSV text" fallback; loaded-file card (name, rows×cols, numeric count, remove ✕, ≥20-row warning); column pickers (regression: Feature/Target from numeric cols; classification: x₁/x₂ + Label from all cols; "Auto ·" sentinels); samples/noise/seed controls hidden in csv mode (replaced by rows box); `run()`/`previewDataset()` attach csv+columns; train/preview/legend/meta/history/aria copy all source-aware (e.g. "class 0 · active (test)", "61 rows from reg_test.csv · 49 train · 12 test · 2 dropped").
- BUG FOUND+FIXED during testing: strict "every cell numeric" column test → one junk row disqualified ALL columns ("found 0 numeric"). Switched to ≥90% parseable threshold; junk rows now counted as `droppedRows`.
- Also fixed: preview table row key `row.i` → first-column-based (csv index column may be `i#`); classification chart aria-label for csv runs.

Verification (all green):
- `bun run lint`: 0 errors/warnings.
- curl API matrix: reg CSV preview (61/63 usable, r=0.8135, trend absent) & train (r²=0.6187, ridge+explicit weight_kg feature works, bad pick falls back to auto); cls CSV train (acc 0.95, labelMap active/inactive, 3600-pt boundary) & preview (50/50 balance); errors: too-few-rows, no-binary-label (clear 400 detail); `;`-delimited CSV sniffed (r²=0.9998); determinism: identical test points across identical POSTs.
- Synthetic path unchanged: seed 42 → r² 0.9121 (historical value), preview cols [i,x,y], trend present.
- agent-browser E2E: file upload via real input; client-side parse (63 rows × 3 cols toast); preview table shows dose_mg/clearance_Lh + "2 malformed rows dropped"; Train → metric cards R² 0.6187 / RMSE 0.1672 / MAE 0.1432 + 5-fold CV 0.5319±0.208 + fitted-curve chart + "61 rows from reg_test.csv" (VLM-verified); classification E2E: label-less CSV → actionable alert; cls_test.csv → acc 0.9375, legends "class 0 · active (test)"/"class 1 · inactive (test)", boundary chart VLM-verified; paste path: loaded as pasted.csv, regression r²=0.9998; sessionStorage state inspected (source=csv, result.source correct, history csv metadata); 360px mobile: no overflow/overlap, toggles side-by-side, file row readable; dev.log: 0 errors; zero hydration issues.

Work Log:
- Read worklog Task IDs 18/20/19 (round conventions: rar-parity upgrades, report audits, CLI handler pattern, docs regen discipline) and the legacy scripts: `Convert SMI to CSV.py` (line-by-line .smi streaming with utf-8-sig BOM, no in-memory list), `Convert CSV to SDF.py` (per-row MolFromSmiles → AddHs → ETKDG → UFFOptimizeMolecule → `ligand_{i+1}` title → SDWriter; invalid SMILES printed and skipped) and `others/Ml/Sdf reading.ipynb` (PandasTools.LoadSDF + `df['SMILES'] = df.ROMol.apply(Chem.MolToSmiles)`).
- RDKit API probes on 2026.09.1 before writing code: ETKDG params + fixed `randomSeed` via `AllChem.EmbedMolecule(mol, params)`; UFFOptimizeMolecule returns 0=converged; `GetPropsAsDict(includePrivate=False, includeComputed=False)` round-trips numeric SD tags as numbers; SDMolSupplier raises OSError on empty/missing files and yields None records on junk; SDWriter auto-computes 2D coordinates for conformer-less molecules (the graceful "2D/no-coordinate" fallback); SDMolSupplier strips explicit Hs on read so canonical SMILES come back clean. Probed 11 pathological SMILES (cubane, prismane, valinomycin, multi-spiro …) for genuine ETKDG failures — modern ETKDG embeds them all, so the fallback path is tested by simulating the -1 return code.
- NEW in src/pharmaml/chem/converters.py (+~450 lines, module docstring extended to contrast the three rar sources; `csv` import, DependencyError import, __all__ +5): private `_require_rdkit()` (lazy, descriptors.py-style "pip install 'pharmaml[chem]' (or pip install rdkit)" hint), `_iter_smi_lines` streaming parser, `_cell_text` (NaN/blank → None, numeric ".0"-strip, mirroring ligands._row_name), `_load_conversion_table` (DataFrame | CSV/TSV/Excel path via utils.io.read_table, "<dataframe>" source marker). Public API:
  * `smi_to_csv(smi_path, out_csv=None) -> Path` — streams rows as they are read (rar memory parity), columns SMILES/ID with whitespace-joined multi-word IDs (may be empty), blank lines skipped, utf-8-sig BOM output, default `<stem>.csv` beside the input, n-rows summary log, missing file → DataValidationError.
  * `table_to_sdf(data, out_sdf, *, smiles_col="SMILES", name_col=None, embed3d=True, uff=True, seed=0xF10E) -> SdfWriteReport` — every non-SMILES column becomes an SD tag (NaN cells simply not written); `_Name` = name_col value with 1-based-row-number fallback for empty/NaN cells, else rar-parity `ligand_{i}`; AddHs + ETKDG with fixed seed; embedding failure non-fatal (molecule kept without coordinates + note; UFF skipped); UFF convergence check non-fatal (note only); invalid SMILES counted + kept in `failed_smiles` (LigandLibrary accounting parity) and never written; scoped RDLogger suppression so parse errors land in the report instead of raw stderr; all-invalid or empty table → DataValidationError; rdkit missing → DependencyError.
  * `SdfWriteReport` @dataclass(slots=True) — source, output_sdf, molecules, failures, smiles_column, embedded_3d, notes, failed_smiles + LigandPreparationReport-style aligned `__str__` audit ("Table -> SDF conversion", note lines appended).
  * `sdf_to_table(sdf_path, out=None, smiles_col="SMILES") -> pd.DataFrame` — the Sdf reading.ipynb upgrade without PandasTools: one row per molecule with canonical SMILES, title → "name" column, every SD property as a typed column (SMILES+name ordered first); OSError → "empty or unreadable SDF" DataValidationError; all-None records → "no parseable molecules"; optional `out` written via write_table + logged.
  * `table_to_smi(data, out_smi, *, smiles_col="SMILES", name_col=None) -> Path` — inverse of smi_to_csv: "<smiles> <name>" lines, name omitted when no name_col or empty cell, blank SMILES cells skipped like blank .smi lines, validation mirrors smi_to_csv.
- Exported the 4 functions + SdfWriteReport from chem/__init__.py (same pattern as add_pic50_column; first docstring line kept a complete sentence so the INDEX blurb stays whole); chem blurb now "endpoint and file-format conversion, descriptors, similarity".
- CLI (src/pharmaml/cli.py): new `convert` subcommand under `data` — `--data` (.smi / .csv/.tsv/.xlsx / .sdf), `--to {csv,sdf,smi}`, `--smiles-col` (default SMILES), `--name-col` (default None → ligand_N titles), `--no-3d` (skip embed+UFF, table→sdf only), `--out` (default = input stem + target extension). `_cmd_data_convert` dispatches on (suffix, target): .smi→csv = smi_to_csv; →sdf = table_to_sdf(embed3d=not --no-3d) with a CLI-internal `_smi_bridge_frame` (.smi → SMILES/ID frame, so .smi→sdf works without the legacy two-step temp CSV); →smi = table_to_smi; .sdf→csv = sdf_to_table(out=...); .sdf→smi chains sdf_to_table + table_to_smi with automatic "name" column reuse; table→csv re-emits via read_table/write_table (xlsx/tsv shuffle). Bad combos are clear DataValidationErrors: .sdf→sdf ("already an SDF file -- use --to csv or --to smi"), .smi→smi. Prints the full SdfWriteReport audit + "output -> path" for sdf targets (failures → stderr note + exit 1, prep-ligands precedent) and compact "converted a -> b (n …)" lines otherwise. Registered ("data", "convert") in _HANDLERS; module docstring fence gains `pharmaml data convert --data ligands.csv --to sdf`.
- Tests: NEW tests/test_format_converters.py — 41 tests (importorskip rdkit gate, real SMILES fixtures CCO/c1ccccc1/aspirin/not_a_smiles): smi_to_csv (multi-word IDs joined, blank lines skipped, BOM bytes, missing file, custom out), table_to_sdf (DataFrame + CSV path sources, audit `__str__` alignment, SD-tag round-trip with typed values, name_col naming + empty-cell row-number fallback, default ligand_N naming, invalid accounting failures+failed_smiles, embed3d=False fast path still valid SDF, simulated ETKDG failure → fallback note + molecule still written, UFF non-convergence note, uff=False never calls UFF, all-invalid/empty-frame/missing-column errors, DependencyError via monkeypatch sys.modules {"rdkit": None, "rdkit.Chem": None}), sdf_to_table (out= writes table, missing/empty/unparseable SDF errors, DependencyError), table_to_smi (line format, name omitted without name_col, empty-name and blank-SMILES cells, error paths), CLI (csv→sdf --no-3d happy path with audit assertions, default out-path derivation, smi→csv, .smi→sdf bridge with --name-col ID, sdf→csv, csv→smi, invalid-SMILES exit 1 + stderr note, .sdf→sdf rejected, missing file exit 1). One real bug found by the tests and fixed: empty name_col cells fell back to `ligand_{i}` instead of the specified row number.
- Docs: scratch/build_docs_index.py CLI_SECTION fence gains `data convert` (round-20 precedent); regenerated docs/INDEX.md → 9 modules / 128 symbols (123 → +5 chem symbols; the regen also carries the parallel agent's in-flight `plot_receptor_atom_types` so the drift guard stays green). test_docs_index 22/22 pass.

"CON.csv" forms), no trailing-dot/space stripping (Windows silently drops them), and bare-dots names ("."/".."/"...") passed through.   Cosmetic: trailing separator left behind by stripped trailing dots ("ligand . " → "ligand_" before, "ligand" now).

Task ID: 100x-18-b
Agent: general-purpose subagent
Task: Round 18/1000 (sub-task b) — adversarial deep review of ml/neural.py + its dnn/dnn_residual registry wiring (built by 100x-17-a; only the builder had reviewed it): find REAL defects, fix minimally, add fail-first regression tests.

Work Log:
- Read the worklog tail (100x-07 … 100x-17-a/b for the verify-before-fix + fail-first conventions), then ml/neural.py (981 LOC) line by line, the dnn/dnn_residual entries + _TASK_FACTORIES in models.py, ml/__init__.py exports, tests/test_neural.py (57 tests) + tests/test_ml_pipeline.py consumers, and training.py's param_grid flow. Baseline confirmed pre-work: test_neural.py 57/57, test_ml_pipeline.py 46 passed + 1 skipped.
- Wrote 4 scratch probes (scratch/probe_neural_{1_earlystop,2_split,3_errors,4_registry}.py, 405 LOC total) and ran every suspicion empirically BEFORE touching code; pristine copies kept in /tmp/task18b for the fail-first round-trip. 7 defects reproduced, all fixed minimally in neural.py (models.py registry + ml/__init__.py verified CLEAN — no change):
  1. HIGH — predict with FEWER feature columns than fitted silently BROADCAST: (n,1) X minus the (2,) per-feature mean pads to (n,2) in NumPy, so a 1-feature input returned full-shape GARBAGE predictions with no error (probe: 2-feature model, predict(X[:, :1]) -> (90,) output, all class 0); too MANY columns escaped as a raw numpy ValueError ("operands could not be broadcast"). Fix: _forward() now checks X.shape[1] vs n_features_in_ and raises DataValidationError naming both counts (neural.py:681-689).
  2. MED — diverged training left a silently-NaN estimator: DeepMLPRegressor(learning_rate=1e9) fit "successfully" and predict() returned all-NaN (probe: preds=[nan nan nan]); with lr=1e6 weights stayed finite only because best-epoch restore rescued them. Fix: after training, all stored weights are checked for finiteness and fit raises ModelError "training diverged — ... lower learning_rate" BEFORE history_/n_iter_/_weights are assigned, so the refused estimator predicts nothing (neural.py:624-633).
  3. MED — stratified val split stole single-member classes: _train_val_split only capped the per-class holdout when members.size > 1, so with val_split>=~0.6 a class with exactly ONE row landed entirely in the validation fold — the class vanished from TRAINING (probe: 3-class set, val_split=0.6 -> train classes {0,1}), violating the documented "every class keeps at least one training row". Fix: unconditional cap k = min(k, max(members.size - 1, 0)) (neural.py:487-489).
  4. MED — standardisation leaked the validation fold: _mean_/_std_ were computed over ALL rows passed to fit (comment claimed "fitted on the training block only"); probe planted a x500 outlier that landed in the VAL fold and dragged _mean_[0] from -0.18 (train-only) to +12.31 (full-data) — the early-stopping metric was computed on rows the scaler had seen. Fix: the split (seeded rng) now happens in fit() BEFORE scaling and mean/std use X_arr[train_idx] only; train_idx/val_idx/rng are threaded into _train_torch so the whole fit stays one deterministic random stream (neural.py:429-445).
  5. MED — y containing None escaped fit() as a RAW TypeError ("'<' not supported between instances of 'NoneType' and 'str'") from np.unique's sort. Fix: label encoding wrapped -> DataValidationError "classification targets could not be label-encoded ..." (neural.py:386-392).
  6. MED — a FAILED fit (torch missing) left a half-fitted estimator: classes_/n_features_in_/_mean_/_std_ were written before _require_torch(), so sklearn's check_is_fitted considered the estimator FITTED while predict raised "not fitted" (probe with sys.modules['torch']=None). Fix: torch import + device resolution moved before ANY fitted attribute is written (neural.py:417-424).
  7. LOW-MED — hidden_layers passed as a GENERATOR silently degenerated to a single linear layer: _validate_hyperparameters' loop consumed the generator, so _train_torch's tuple(...) saw () (probe: gen for (16,8) -> _net_spec_['hidden'] == ()). Fix: validation materialises hidden_layers ONCE and returns the tuple, which fit() threads through to _train_torch (neural.py:248, 282, 370, 507).
  8. LOW docstring drift — _ensure_network claimed the live module is None "after unpickling (or a set_params mutation)"; set_params actually keeps the OLD live network (behaviour pinned by the existing test_set_params_after_fit_keeps_predictions_working) -> docstring corrected; fit()'s Raises section updated for the new diverged-training/feature-count paths.
- Hunt categories that came up EMPTY after probing (no defect, no invented fix): best-weight restore IS correct (probe: stored weights == weights at the epoch of minimum val loss, verified via the train-twice-same-seed trick); patience semantics off-by-one absent (stops exactly on the Nth stale epoch); n_iter_ == epochs RUN as documented; refit resets history_ (never appends); dropout correctly OFF at predict (two predict_proba calls bit-identical at dropout=0.5); batch_size > n_samples and the last partial batch fine; mixed-dtype/int/float32 X handled with clear errors; classes_ sorted and predict_proba columns EXACTLY aligned (the multiclass ['b','a','c'] pin — my first probe expectation was wrong: the label permutation puts 'a' at the (0,4) centre; with enough epochs all three centres classify correctly with argmax in the right columns); tiny-n regression split (n=2 -> 1/1, n=1 -> no split); classifier float-y NaN -> typed error; val_split fraction semantics + [0,1) boundary; registry param_grid values all settable (8 combos, keys ⊆ constructor params), all 7 alias spellings resolve, task='both' dispatch works for train_model/compare_models (existing tests), torch-missing registry path (existing _hide_torch_spec tests); GridSearchCV determinism (two identical searches -> identical best_score_/best_params_/predictions; no DataLoader exists — shuffling uses the seeded numpy rng).
- Flagged, deliberately NOT fixed: (a) _forward calls out.numpy() on the LIVE network — on a CUDA-fitted model (device='cuda') the live net sits on GPU and both the input tensor (created on CPU) and .numpy() would raise raw torch errors; cannot demonstrate without CUDA in this sandbox, so no code change (one-line .detach().cpu().numpy() if ever needed); (b) no feature-NAME consistency check at predict (sklearn warns; ours is silent) — a warning-feature, not a defect; (c) no gradient clipping — not claimed anywhere, GIGO for extreme-but-finite learning rates stays possible (finite garbage predictions at lr=1e6 are user-set hyperparams, the NaN case is now guarded); (d) classes_/n_features_in_ still linger after a DIVERGED fit (set before training) — predict stays safe ("not fitted") but check_is_fitted sees classes_; full transactional fit would need a try/except around all of fit, judged scope creep; (e) clone() of a generator-param estimator re-passes the exhausted generator (inherent sklearn trap with iterable params, the original fit is now correct).
- +12 regression tests appended to tests/test_neural.py as three new classes (TestEarlyStoppingRegression x4, TestValidationSplitRegression x2, TestInputContractRegression x6). Fail-first verification via pristine-copy round-trip (/tmp/task18b): 7/12 FAILED against pre-fix sources (diverged-training DID NOT RAISE; single-member class missing from train fold; _mean_ == full-data mean; raw broadcast ValueError on 13-feature predict; raw TypeError on None labels; generator -> hidden == (); classes_ present after failed fit) — 5/12 are DISCLOSED pinning tests for behaviour that was already correct and the task explicitly asked to pin (best-epoch weight restore via constructed worsening loss curve, small-patience n_iter_ <= epochs, history_ reset on refit, classes_ == ['a','b','c'] with proba column correspondence via centre-argmax, dropout-off determinism). Post-fix: 12/12 pass.
- Verification: `pytest tests/test_neural.py -q -W ignore` -> 69 passed / 0 failed / 0 errors (junitxml; 57 baseline + 12 new), suite runtime ~14s (limit ~90s). `pytest tests/test_ml_pipeline.py -q -W ignore` -> 46 passed + 1 skipped (unchanged). Registry probes re-run post-fix: aliases/determinism/param-grid all clean. End-to-end smoke (scratch/probe_neural_5_smoke.py): DeepMLPClassifier on make_classification(200, 20) accuracy 0.720 @10 epochs / 0.840 @60 epochs (n_iter_ 57, early-stopped) and identical predictions on same-seed refit; ResidualMLPRegressor on sine(x)*cos(y) (200x2) R^2 = 0.635 @10 epochs / 0.920 @120 epochs (early-stopped at n_iter_ 44), score() == r2_score exactly. Full suite NOT run (orchestrator gate); notebooks/README/docs untouched; sibling-owned files (biology/*, cli.py, test_biology.py, test_cli_features.py, models.py classical entries) untouched.

Root cause: regression targets are never zero-centred in pharma (pIC50≈5, LN_IC50≈2-4, AUC∈[0,1]); on raw-scale MSE the output bias crawls toward the target mean at ~learning_rate per step, so early stopping fires before the intercept is learned. Fix in neural.py fit: y z-scaled on training rows only (_y_mean_/_y_std_, constant target keeps std 1), inverted in both regressors' predict. Probe before/after: rmse 4.39 → 0.36 (same data, 60 epochs). +6 tests TestTargetStandardisation (fail-first evidence = pre-fix probe run; pins: pic50-scale learnable rmse<1.0, predictions on target scale, _y_mean_ on training rows, constant target, classifier None attrs, residual regressor <0.5).

  4. MED: generator input (docstring promises "Iterable of SMILES strings") crashed with IndexError — compute_descriptors called list(smiles) TWICE, the second consumption saw []. All three entry points now materialise once via new _as_smiles_list helper.
  5. MED: bare-string input silently exploded into per-character "molecules" (compute_fingerprints('CCO') -> 3x2048 single-atom fingerprints, valid_idx=[0,1,2]). Now a DataValidationError naming a truncated preview of the string; same guard reused by similarity_scan (both args) and top_matches library.
  6. LOW: compute_maccs discarded parse failures with NO warning at all (module docstring promises "explicit invalid-SMILES accounting") — now warns like its siblings; compute_fingerprints' warning now names offending indices (failures[:5]) instead of just a count.

  8. HIGH: similarity_scan used LABEL-based `reference_smiles[ref_pos]` on positional indices — a pd.Series reference with a shuffled integer index attached the WRONG SMILES to every output row (probe: Series(['CCO','CCN','CCF'], index=[1,0,2]) reported 'CCN' with similarity 1.0 computed against 'CCO'), and a Series whose index lacks 0 crashed with KeyError: 0.   9. MED: top_matches with an unparseable query returned a silent EMPTY DataFrame (looks like "no matches"); with '' it returned bogus 0.0-similarity rows. Now raises DataValidationError naming the query.
  10. MED: NaN in float "bit arrays" silently counted as a SET bit (np.asarray([1.0, nan]).astype(bool) -> [True, True]) in tanimoto_from_bitarrays AND tanimoto_matrix — probe: tanimoto 0.5 instead of 0.0/raise). Both now raise on non-finite float input (guard gated on dtype.kind == 'f', zero cost for bool/uint matrices).
  11. LOW: top_matches lacked the radius parameter its wrapper similarity_scan exposes — added radius=2 and forwarded (regression test proves exact equivalence to similarity_scan(..., radius=1).head(k)).
  12. LOW: non-stable quicksort tie ordering made top_matches' k-boundary tie selection arbitrary w.r.t. library order — sort_values(..., kind="stable"); ties now keep library order (pinned).

  1. HIGH pk.py: auc_trapezoid(method="log") implemented the GEOMETRIC-MEAN (midpoint) rule, not the logarithmic trapezoid its docstring names (Purves 1992 reference included) — silent wrong AUC, -19% at a 10x-decay interval (3.1623 vs exact 3.9089) and -53% at 100x (10.0 vs 21.4976). Fixed with the true log rule dt*(c2-c1)/ln(c2/c1); equal-endpoint segments (the 0/0 corner) fall back to the linear rule, which is exact for constant C. Docstring updated to state the segment integral and the fallback. Notebook impact: complete_pk_workflow.ipynb §3's committed "AUC log 17.2347" would print 17.2536 (+0.11%) if re-executed — flagged for the orchestrator, notebook untouched per rules.
  2. MED pd.py: the Emax family silently propagated NaN concentrations (nan -> nan effect) and even turned inf into inf/inf = NaN with a numpy RuntimeWarning; a single NaN element poisoned one effect_table row. New shared _validated_concentration guard (finite + non-negative) in emax/sigmoid/inhibitory; linear_effect deliberately left as pure algebra (no documented domain constraint).
  3. MED dosage.py: tdm_trough_adjustment's non-linear-kinetics guard was ONE-SIDED — target/measured > 5 (escalations) raised, but a 10x reduction (1000 mg -> 100 mg) sailed through silently although the docstring's own rationale (non-steady-state sampling / non-linearity / data entry error) applies symmetrically. Guard now enforces ratio in [1/5, 5]; message names the ratio; existing 2x/3x-reduction tests unaffected.
  4. MED units.py: the _parse_formula_group docstring example "Co(NH3)6.3H2O also parse correctly" RAISED — Co was missing from _ATOMIC_WEIGHTS. Added Co 58.933 (IUPAC abridged); verified 215.164 g/mol.
  5. MED units.py: malformed formulas silently accepted — "Ca()2" -> 40.078 (empty group = 0) and "MgSO4.7" -> 120.361 (dangling hydrate count adds nothing) — despite "malformed groups raise". Both now raise UnitConversionError with the offending text; valid hydrates (MgSO4.7H2O = 246.466) and groups (Ca(OH)2 = 74.092) pinned unchanged.
  6. MED utils/validation.py (pharma public API re-export, also feeds viz + CLI): junk text in a time/conc column escaped as a RAW numpy ValueError ("could not convert string to float: 'blq'") instead of the documented DataValidationError; `pharmaml pharma pk` on a CSV with a text cell surfaced that raw message.   8. LOW pk.py: iv_bolus_concentration crashed with raw TypeError on list/tuple/SERIES time input (docstring only promised ndarray, but the package accepts sequences everywhere else). Now dispatches scalar-vs-array via np.ndim; float-in -> float-out preserved.

  10. LOW pd.py: ec50_for_target_effect(target == e0) raised "unreachable" — factually wrong (the baseline effect is reached at C = 0). Boundary relaxed to f in [0, 1): returns 0.0; f -> 1 (C -> inf) still rejected; docstring + error text updated.

  12. LOW docstring truth fixes: nca_summary claimed "last two (or three) positive points by log-linear regression" (code: exactly-two secant) — now says last-two secant + notes BLQ-zero exclusion; oral_tmax claimed "flip-flop kinetics make the formula invalid" (the closed form is symmetric in ka/ke and stays valid) — now states the refusal is deliberate conservatism against data-entry mistakes; renal_dose_adjustment said "Linearly reduce a dose" while CrCl ABOVE normal escalates the dose (150% at CrCl 150/100) — first line now "Linearly scale a dose with renal function" + bidirectional explanation; docs/INDEX.md regenerated (single row changed; test_docs_index green, no new public symbols so no new rows).

STRETCH cli.py (_cmd_chem_descriptors, round-19 triage): frame.join(descriptors) aligned on the caller frame's INDEX LABELS while descriptors are indexed by POSITION in the SMILES column — any frame without a default RangeIndex silently attached the wrong row's descriptors (fail-first repro with index=[1,0]: the CCO row carried CCN's MolWt 45.08 instead of 46.07). RL variant = REINFORCE with running EMA baseline + per-batch advantage normalization + entropy bonus + squared-drift prior anchor (mode-collapse brake; anchor_weight=0 recovers vanilla REINFORCE-with-baseline) + top-score experience replay + gradient clipping + plateau early stop — documented honestly as NOT the original REINVENT augmented-likelihood formulation. Determinism strategy: GA = dedicated random.Random(seed); RNN = torch.manual_seed at model build + dedicated torch.Generator objects for shuffle and sampling; RL = per-iteration sampling seeds (seed*1_000_003 + iteration mod 2^31) so any caller-side sampling of the prior cannot shift a run; cross-process equality verified. RDKit AND torch both lazy (actionable DependencyError with pip install 'pharmaml[chem]'/'pharmaml[deep]' hints at call time; import-time safety test-enforced for both halves).
- Suite: 1079 passed / 2 skipped / 0 failed in ~32s (baseline 993/~25s; generative adds ~10s — one module-scoped 8-epoch char-GRU prior < 1s + GA/RL smoke budgets sized for the sandbox).
